251:GATE-B PARTIAL-FAIL (exactly as cond theory predicts, the bigger problem than expected): CholeskyQR2 forms G=A^T A which SQUARES cond; fp32 Cholesky breaks down for cond(A) > u^-1/2 ~ 4096. In fp64 this only bites the ill-cond cases (rankdef/nearrank/nearcollinear/mixed break; clustered/band/rowscale pass). But in fp32 (the precision GATE-C needs) the squared cond breaks Cholesky on the DENSE BENCHMARK SHAPES THEMSELVES -- n512 b640 cond2, n1024, n2048, n4096, even n176 -- because random-Gaussian cond ~ n already exceeds 4096 once squared. Shifted CholeskyQR3 chol(G+sI) recovers most dense shapes but STILL fails the DOMINANT n512 b640 cond2 without a shift so large it then violates orthogonality. So the fallback fraction is not just the ill-cond cases; it's most of the batch at the dominant shapes.
